Tag may be '{uri}name' or plain 'name'\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n        return tag.rsplit(\"}\", 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\ndef read_json(path: Path) -> dict:\n    with path.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\n\ndef format_date_yyyy_mm_dd_to_dots(s: str) -> str:\n    # Replace any YYYY-MM-DD occurrences with YYYY.MM.DD\n    return re.sub(r\"(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})\", r\"\\1.\\2.\\3\", s)\n\n\ndef risk_with_severity(raw: str) -> str:\n    mapping = {\n        \"High\": \"즉시조치\",\n        \"Medium\": \"계획보완\",\n        \"Low\": \"모니터링\",\n    }\n    note = mapping.get(raw)\n    if not note:\n        return raw\n    return f\"{raw} {note}\"\n\n\ndef build_replacements(overview: dict, actions: dict) -> Tuple[Dict[str, str], str, str]:\n    summary = overview.get(\"summary\") or {}\n\n    inspection_date_raw = str(summary.get(\"점검일\", \"\"))\n    inspection_date_dot = format_date_yyyy_mm_dd_to_dots(inspection_date_raw)\n\n    risk_raw = str(summary.get(\"위험등급\", \"\"))\n    risk_full = risk_with_severity(risk_raw)\n\n    replacements: Dict[str, str] = {}\n\n    for k, v in summary.items():\n        key = str(k)\n        val = \"\" if v is None else str(v)\n        if key == \"점검일\":\n            val = inspection_date_dot\n        elif key == \"위험등급\":\n            val = risk_full\n        replacements[f\"{{{{{key}}}}}\"] = val\n\n    immediate = actions.get(\"immediate_actions\") or []\n    if not isinstance(immediate, list):\n        raise ValueError(\"corrective_actions.json: immediate_actions must be a list\")\n\n    # Fill first three lines in order. If fewer provided, fill missing with empty.\n    for i in range(3):\n        val = \"\"\n        if i < len(immediate):\n            val = \"\" if immediate[i] is None else str(immediate[i])\n        replacements[f\"{{{{조치{i+1}}}}}\"] = val\n\n    return replacements, inspection_date_raw, risk_raw\n\n\ndef discover_namespaces(xml_path: Path) -> Dict[str, str]:\n    ns: Dict[str, str] = {}\n    # iterparse with 'start-ns' yields (prefix, uri)\n    for event, item in ET.iterparse(str(xml_path), events=(\"start-ns\",)):\n        prefix, uri = item\n        # Keep first occurrence of each prefix\n        if prefix not in ns:\n            ns[prefix] = uri\n    return ns\n\n\ndef register_namespaces(ns: Dict[str, str]) -> None:\n    for prefix, uri in ns.items():\n        # ElementTree reserves '' for default namespace.\n        ET.register_namespace(prefix, uri)\n\n\ndef iter_paragraphs(root: ET.Element) -> Iterable[ET.Element]:\n    for el in root.iter():\n        if localname(el.tag) == \"p\":\n            yield el\n\n\ndef iter_text_nodes(paragraph: ET.Element) -> List[ET.Element]:\n    t_nodes: List[ET.Element] = []\n    for el in paragraph.iter():\n        if localname(el.tag) == \"t\":\n            t_nodes.append(el)\n    return t_nodes\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache(paragraph: ET.Element, parent_map: Dict[ET.Element, ET.Element]) -> int:\n    removed = 0\n    # Remove both <hp:lineSegArray> and <hp:linesegarray> variants if present.\n    for el in list(paragraph.iter()):\n        ln = localname(el.tag)\n        if ln in (\"lineSegArray\", \"linesegarray\"):\n            parent = parent_map.get(el)\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(el)\n                removed += 1\n    return removed\n\n\ndef apply_replacements_to_xml(xml_path: Path, replacements: Dict[str, str], inspection_date_raw: str, risk_raw: str) -> None:\n    ns = discover_namespaces(xml_path)\n    register_namespaces(ns)\n\n    tree = ET.parse(str(xml_path))\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    # Parent pointers needed to remove layout cache elements\n    parent_map: Dict[ET.Element, ET.Element] = {c: p for p in root.iter() for c in p}\n\n    modified_paragraphs = 0\n    removed_caches = 0\n\n    risk_full = risk_with_severity(risk_raw) if risk_raw else \"\"\n\n    # A regex that replaces the raw risk tier only when it isn't already suffixed.\n    # We do this post-placeholder replacement to catch both placeholder and literal occurrences.\n    risk_re: Optional[re.Pattern[str]] = None\n    if risk_raw and risk_full and risk_raw != risk_full:\n        # Match as a standalone token (not embedded in an identifier) and not already followed by known suffix.\n        # Example matches: 'High', 'High,' 'High)'\n        suffixes = \"즉시조치|계획보완|모니터링\"\n        risk_re = re.compile(\n            rf\"(?<![A-Za-z0-9_]){re.escape(risk_raw)}(?![A-Za-z0-9_])(?!(?:\\\\s*(?:{suffixes})))\"\n        )\n\n    for p in iter_paragraphs(root):\n        t_nodes = iter_text_nodes(p)\n        if not t_nodes:\n            continue\n\n        original_text = \"\".join((t.text or \"\") for t in t_nodes)\n        if not original_text:\n            continue\n\n        new_text = original_text\n\n        # Replace placeholders\n        for placeholder, value in replacements.items():\n            if placeholder in new_text:\n                new_text = new_text.replace(placeholder, value)\n\n        # Normalize dates everywhere (including within replaced fields)\n        new_text = format_date_yyyy_mm_dd_to_dots(new_text)\n\n        # 레이아웃 캐시(lineSegArray/linesegarray) 제거\n치환으로 **텍스트가 변경된 모든 `<hp:p>`**에 대해:\n- `<hp:lineSegArray>` 또는 `<hp:linesegarray>` 노드를 제거하도록 처리했습니다.\n- (템플릿에는 `linesegarray` 형태로 들어있었고, 수정된 문단에서는 제거된 상태로 저장됨)\n\n### 6) 플레이스홀더 잔존 검사\n최종 HWPX 내부의 모든 `.xml`에서 `{{...}}` 패턴이 남아있는지 검사했고:\n\n- `Contents/section0.xml: clean`\n- `Contents/section1.xml: clean`\n\n으로 **잔존 플레이스홀더가 없음을 확인**했습니다.\n\n### 7) 산출물
